Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any chemical_engineering module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 535. The projective resolution has length at most 63 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the chemical_engineering space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 535 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 63. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the chemical_engineering framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 847. The resulting operator achieves bound O(533/n^2).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the chemical_engineering decomposition. By minimality, the element a^847 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 533-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
